################################################################################
## DATEI: ./main.js
################################################################################

``````javascript
// main.js - VLCopyCat / CopyCat Media Player Main Process
// Modular Architecture with Dependency Injection:
//   - src/main/container.js      -> Lightweight IoC Service Container & WindowManager
//   - src/main/stream-manager.js  -> Dedicated stream & thumbnail process manager
//   - src/main/logger.js         -> Structured, prefixed logging
//   - src/main/session.js        -> Windows OS integrations & AI sandbox breakout
//   - src/main/updater.js        -> GitHub Releases auto-updater & asset downloader
//   - src/main/media-resolver.js -> Media detection, sniffer, probing & folder indexing
//   - src/main/ipc.js            -> Declarative IPC registry with injected services
//   - src/main/server.js         -> Local HTTP AI control server with dynamic command registry

const { app, BrowserWindow, protocol, net, Menu, shell } = require('electron');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { pathToFileURL } = require('url');

// 1. Structured Logging Module
const { createLogger } = require('./src/main/logger');
const mainLogger = createLogger('Main');

// 2. Windows Session & AI Sandbox Breakout
const { handleAiSandboxBreakout } = require('./src/main/session');

// Trigger breakout if running inside AI agent terminal sandboxes
handleAiSandboxBreakout();

// 3. High-Performance Hardware Acceleration & Zero-Copy Compositing
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-gpu-rasterization');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-zero-copy');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('ignore-gpu-blocklist');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('disable-background-timer-throttling');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-accelerated-video-decode');
app.commandLine.appendSwitch('enable-accelerated-mjpeg-decode');

protocol.registerSchemesAsPrivileged([
  {
    scheme: 'media',
    privileges: {
      standard: true,
      secure: true,
      supportFetchAPI: true,
      corsEnabled: true,
      stream: true,
      bypassCSP: true
    }
  }
]);

process.on('uncaughtException', (err) => {
  mainLogger.error('uncaughtException:', err.stack || err.message);
});
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => {
  mainLogger.error('unhandledRejection:', reason?.stack || reason);
});

// 4. Initialize Dependency Injection Service Container
const { createAppContainer } = require('./src/main/container');
const container = createAppContainer();

const windowManager = container.get('window');
const streamManager = container.get('stream');
const mediaService = container.get('media');
const sessionService = container.get('session');
const updaterService = container.get('updater');

// 5. Connect Declarative IPC Registry with Injected Services
const { registerIpcRegistry } = require('./src/main/ipc');
registerIpcRegistry(container);

// 6. Start Local HTTP AI Control Server (Port 4500)
const { startAiControlServer } = require('./src/main/server');

// 7. Window Creation & Lifecycle Setup
function createMainWindow() {
  const icoPath = path.join(__dirname, 'assets', 'icon.ico');
  const iconPng = path.join(__dirname, 'assets', 'icon.png');
  const windowIcon = fs.existsSync(icoPath) ? icoPath : (fs.existsSync(iconPng) ? iconPng : undefined);

  const mainWindow = new BrowserWindow({
    width: 1180,
    height: 700,
    minWidth: 680,
    minHeight: 420,
    frame: false,
    backgroundColor: '#0A0A0A',
    title: 'CopyCat media player',
    icon: windowIcon,
    show: false,
    webPreferences: {
      preload: path.join(__dirname, 'preload.js'),
      contextIsolation: true,
      nodeIntegration: false,
      webSecurity: false,
      backgroundThrottling: false
    }
  });

  mainWindow.loadFile(path.join(__dirname, 'src', 'index.html'));

  mainWindow.webContents.on('console-message', (event, level, message, line, sourceId) => {
    if (level >= 2 || process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
      mainLogger.debug(`[Renderer ${level}] ${message} (${sourceId}:${line})`);
    }
  });

  mainWindow.webContents.setWindowOpenHandler(({ url }) => {
    if (url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('http://')) {
      shell.openExternal(url);
    }
    return { action: 'deny' };
  });

  // Attach all window lifecycle and state events cleanly via WindowManager
  windowManager.attachWindow(mainWindow);

  return mainWindow;
}

// 8. Single Instance Lock & App Initialization
const isSingleInstance = app.requestSingleInstanceLock();

if (!isSingleInstance) {
  app.quit();
} else {
  const initialMedia = mediaService.resolveCliFile(process.argv);
  if (initialMedia) {
    windowManager.setInitialMedia(initialMedia);
  }

  app.on('second-instance', (_event, commandLine) => {
    const win = windowManager.getWindow();
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      windowManager.bringToFront(win);
      const payload = mediaService.resolveCliFile(commandLine);
      if (payload) {
        windowManager.setInitialMedia(payload);
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', payload);
      }
    }
  });

  app.whenReady().then(() => {
    Menu.setApplicationMenu(null);

    protocol.handle('media', (request) => {
      try {
        let pathname = decodeURIComponent(request.url.replace(/^media:\/\/\/?/i, ''));
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && /^\/[a-zA-Z]:/.test(pathname)) {
          pathname = pathname.slice(1);
        }
        return net.fetch(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(pathname)).href);
      } catch (err) {
        mainLogger.warn('Media protocol error:', err.message);
        return new Response('Media file not found', { status: 404 });
      }
    });

    startAiControlServer(4500, container);
    createMainWindow();

    setTimeout(() => {
      sessionService.registerAssociations(process.execPath);
    }, 1500);

    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const updateInfo = await updaterService.check();
        const win = windowManager.getWindow();
        if (updateInfo && updateInfo.updateAvailable && win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
          win.webContents.send('updater:available', updateInfo);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        mainLogger.debug('Update check skipped:', err.message);
      }
    }, 2500);
  });
}

app.on('before-quit', () => {
  streamManager.killAll();
});

app.on('window-all-closed', () => {
  streamManager.killAll();
  if (process.platform !== 'darwin') app.quit();
});
``````

################################################################################
## DATEI: ./src/main/container.js
################################################################################

``````javascript
// src/main/container.js
// Lightweight Service Container & Dependency Injection Registry for VLCopyCat

const { app } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');
const mediaResolver = require('./media-resolver');
const sessionModule = require('./session');
const updaterModule = require('./updater');
const { createStreamManager } = require('./stream-manager');

const logger = createLogger('Container');

/**
 * Lightweight Service Container (IoC)
 */
class ServiceContainer {
  constructor() {
    this._services = new Map();
    this._factories = new Map();
  }

  register(name, instance) {
    this._services.set(name, instance);
    return this;
  }

  factory(name, factoryFn) {
    this._factories.set(name, factoryFn);
    return this;
  }

  get(name) {
    if (this._services.has(name)) {
      return this._services.get(name);
    }
    if (this._factories.has(name)) {
      const instance = this._factories.get(name)(this);
      this._services.set(name, instance);
      return instance;
    }
    throw new Error(`Service "${name}" is not registered in the ServiceContainer.`);
  }

  has(name) {
    return this._services.has(name) || this._factories.has(name);
  }

  getAll() {
    const all = {};
    for (const [key, value] of this._services.entries()) {
      all[key] = value;
    }
    for (const key of this._factories.keys()) {
      if (!all[key]) {
        all[key] = this.get(key);
      }
    }
    return all;
  }
}

/**
 * Window & Playback State Manager Service
 */
class WindowManager {
  constructor() {
    this.mainWindow = null;
    this.aiServerPort = 4500;
    this.userAlwaysOnTop = false;
    this.isInPipMode = false;
    this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    this.restoringFromPipLeave = false;
    this.lastPipBounds = null;
    this.pendingInitialMedia = null;

    this.latestPlayerStatus = {
      app: 'CopyCat media player',
      playing: false,
      title: null,
      filePath: null,
      currentTime: 0,
      duration: 0,
      volume: 100,
      muted: false,
      speed: 1,
      accent: 'orange',
      queueLength: 0,
      streamEngine: 'native'
    };
  }

  getWindow() {
    return this.mainWindow;
  }

  setWindow(win) {
    this.mainWindow = win;
  }

  isAvailable() {
    return Boolean(this.mainWindow && !this.mainWindow.isDestroyed());
  }

  /**
   * Bind all window lifecycle and state events (clean separation from main.js)
   * @param {BrowserWindow} win
   */
  attachWindow(win) {
    this.mainWindow = win;

    const forceShowTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.isAvailable() && !win.isVisible()) {
        this.bringToFront(win);
        this.broadcastState();
      }
    }, 1200);

    win.once('ready-to-show', () => {
      clearTimeout(forceShowTimer);
      this.bringToFront(win);
      this.broadcastState();
      this.updateThumbar(false);

      const initial = this.getInitialMedia();
      if (initial) {
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', initial);
      }
    });

    win.webContents.on('did-finish-load', () => {
      const initial = this.getInitialMedia();
      if (initial) {
        win.webContents.send('media:initial', initial);
      }
    });

    win.on('maximize', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('unmaximize', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('enter-full-screen', () => this.broadcastState());
    win.on('leave-full-screen', () => this.broadcastState());

    win.on('minimize', () => {
      this.broadcastState();
      if (this.isAvailable()) {
        win.webContents.send('win:minimize');
      }
    });

    win.on('restore', () => {
      this.broadcastState();
      if (this.isAvailable()) {
        win.webContents.send('win:restore');
      }
      if (this.isInPipMode && !this.restoringFromPipLeave && this.isAvailable()) {
        this.isInPipMode = false;
        win.webContents.send('pip:exitRequested');
        if (this.wasFullscreenBeforePip) {
          this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
          win.setFullScreen(true);
        }
      }
    });

    logger.info('Window lifecycle events successfully attached.');
  }

  getPort() {
    return this.aiServerPort;
  }

  setPort(port) {
    this.aiServerPort = port;
  }

  getStatus() {
    return this.latestPlayerStatus;
  }

  setStatus(status) {
    if (status && typeof status === 'object') {
      this.latestPlayerStatus = { ...this.latestPlayerStatus, ...status };
    }
  }

  getInitialMedia() {
    const initial = this.pendingInitialMedia;
    this.pendingInitialMedia = null;
    return initial;
  }

  setInitialMedia(payload) {
    this.pendingInitialMedia = payload;
  }

  getUserAlwaysOnTop() {
    return this.userAlwaysOnTop;
  }

  applyAlwaysOnTop(flag) {
    this.userAlwaysOnTop = Boolean(flag);
    if (this.isAvailable()) {
      try {
        this.mainWindow.setAlwaysOnTop(this.userAlwaysOnTop, 'screen-saver');
      } catch {
        this.mainWindow.setAlwaysOnTop(this.userAlwaysOnTop);
      }
    }
    this.broadcastState();
    return this.userAlwaysOnTop;
  }

  broadcastState() {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return;
    this.mainWindow.webContents.send('win:state', {
      maximized: this.mainWindow.isMaximized(),
      fullscreen: this.mainWindow.isFullScreen(),
      minimized: this.mainWindow.isMinimized(),
      alwaysOnTop: this.userAlwaysOnTop,
      aiPort: this.aiServerPort,
      hasFfmpeg: mediaResolver.hasFfmpeg()
    });
  }

  async enterPip(aspect = 16 / 9) {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return false;
    this.isInPipMode = true;

    if (this.mainWindow.isFullScreen()) {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = true;
      this.mainWindow.setFullScreen(false);
    } else {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    }

    if (!this.mainWindow.isMinimized()) {
      this.mainWindow.minimize();
    }

    const res = await sessionModule.resizePipWindow(this.mainWindow, aspect);
    if (res.bounds) {
      this.lastPipBounds = res.bounds;
    }

    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.isInPipMode && this.isAvailable()) {
        sessionModule.resizePipWindow(this.mainWindow, aspect);
      }
    }, 120);

    this.broadcastState();
    return res.success;
  }

  leavePip(opts = {}) {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return false;
    const wasActive = this.isInPipMode;
    this.isInPipMode = false;
    this.lastPipBounds = null;
    const shouldRestore = opts?.restore !== false;

    if (wasActive && shouldRestore) {
      this.restoringFromPipLeave = true;
      try {
        sessionModule.restoreMainWindowWin32(this.mainWindow);
        if (this.mainWindow.isMinimized()) this.mainWindow.restore();
        this.mainWindow.show();
        this.mainWindow.focus();
        if (this.wasFullscreenBeforePip) {
          this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
          this.mainWindow.setFullScreen(true);
        }
      } finally {
        setTimeout(() => {
          this.restoringFromPipLeave = false;
          this.broadcastState();
        }, 220);
      }
    } else {
      this.wasFullscreenBeforePip = false;
    }

    this.broadcastState();
    return true;
  }

  bringToFront(win = this.mainWindow) {
    sessionModule.bringToFront(win, this.userAlwaysOnTop);
  }

  updateThumbar(isPlaying = false) {
    sessionModule.updateThumbarButtons(this.mainWindow, isPlaying);
  }

  quit() {
    app.quit();
  }
}

/**
 * Initialize and populate the global Dependency Injection Container
 * @returns {ServiceContainer}
 */
function createAppContainer() {
  const container = new ServiceContainer();

  // 1. Core Window & App State Manager
  const windowManager = new WindowManager();
  container.register('window', windowManager);

  // 2. Stream Process Manager (Dedicated process lifecycle for FFmpeg live streams & preview thumbnails)
  const streamManager = createStreamManager();
  container.register('stream', streamManager);

  // 3. Media Resolver Service
  const mediaService = {
    hasFfmpeg: mediaResolver.hasFfmpeg,
    hasFfprobe: mediaResolver.hasFfprobe,
    getFfmpegPath: () => mediaResolver.FFMPEG_PATH,
    getFfprobePath: () => mediaResolver.FFPROBE_PATH,
    probe: mediaResolver.probeMediaFile,
    buildItem: mediaResolver.buildMediaItem,
    scanFolder: mediaResolver.scanMediaFolder,
    findAutoSubtitle: mediaResolver.findAutoSubtitle,
    detectNeedsStream: mediaResolver.detectNeedsStream,
    resolveCliFile: mediaResolver.resolveCliMediaFile,
    MEDIA_EXTENSIONS: mediaResolver.MEDIA_EXTENSIONS,
    SUB_EXTENSIONS: mediaResolver.SUB_EXTENSIONS
  };
  container.register('media', mediaService);

  // 4. Windows Session Service
  const sessionService = {
    hasShortcut: sessionModule.hasDesktopShortcut,
    createShortcut: sessionModule.createDesktopShortcut,
    registerAssociations: sessionModule.runAssociationsRegistration,
    handleBreakout: sessionModule.handleAiSandboxBreakout,
    bringToFront: sessionModule.bringToFront,
    updateThumbar: (isPlaying) => windowManager.updateThumbar(isPlaying)
  };
  container.register('session', sessionService);

  // 5. GitHub Updater Service
  const updaterService = {
    check: updaterModule.checkForUpdates,
    downloadAndInstall: (asset, win) => updaterModule.downloadAndInstallUpdate(asset, win || windowManager.getWindow())
  };
  container.register('updater', updaterService);

  // 6. Structured Logger Service
  container.register('logger', {
    createLogger,
    mainLogger: createLogger('Main')
  });

  logger.info('Service Container initialized with services: window, stream, media, session, updater, logger');
  return container;
}

module.exports = {
  ServiceContainer,
  WindowManager,
  createAppContainer
};
``````

################################################################################
## DATEI: ./src/main/stream-manager.js
################################################################################

``````javascript
// src/main/stream-manager.js
// Dedicated Stream & Thumbnail Worker Process Lifecycle Manager for VLCopyCat

const fs = require('fs');
const { spawn, spawnSync } = require('child_process');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('StreamManager');
const MAX_THUMB_CACHE = 120;

class StreamProcessManager {
  constructor() {
    this.activeStreamProc = null;
    this.activeThumbProc = null;
    this.thumbCache = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Safely terminate active FFmpeg live stream worker
   */
  killActiveStreamProc() {
    if (this.activeStreamProc) {
      try {
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && this.activeStreamProc.pid) {
          spawnSync('taskkill', ['/pid', String(this.activeStreamProc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true });
        } else {
          this.activeStreamProc.kill('SIGKILL');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Stream process kill notice:', err.message);
      }
      this.activeStreamProc = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Safely terminate active FFmpeg thumbnail worker
   */
  killActiveThumbProc() {
    if (this.activeThumbProc) {
      try {
        if (process.platform === 'win32' && this.activeThumbProc.pid) {
          spawnSync('taskkill', ['/pid', String(this.activeThumbProc.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true });
        } else {
          this.activeThumbProc.kill('SIGKILL');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        logger.debug('Thumbnail process kill notice:', err.message);
      }
      this.activeThumbProc = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Kill all background media worker processes
   */
  killAll() {
    this.killActiveStreamProc();
    this.killActiveThumbProc();
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming HTTP /api/stream requests
   * Streams fragmented MP4 over HTTP using FFmpeg
   * @param {URL} urlObj
   * @param {http.IncomingMessage} req
   * @param {http.ServerResponse} res
   * @param {Object} mediaService
   */
  handleStreamRequest(urlObj, req, res, mediaService) {
    const filePath = urlObj.searchParams.get('path') || '';
    const startSec = Math.max(0, Number(urlObj.searchParams.get('start')) || 0);
    const vcodec = (urlObj.searchParams.get('vcodec') || '').toLowerCase();
    const forceTranscode = urlObj.searchParams.get('transcode') === '1';

    const ffmpegPath = mediaService.getFfmpegPath();
    if (!ffmpegPath || !filePath || !fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      res.writeHead(404, { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain; charset=utf-8' });
      res.end('Media file or FFmpeg binary not found');
      return;
    }

    this.killActiveStreamProc();

    const canCopyVideo = !forceTranscode && vcodec === 'h264';
    const args = ['-hide_banner', '-loglevel', 'error'];

    if (startSec > 0.05) {
      args.push('-ss', startSec.toFixed(3));
    }

    args.push('-i', filePath);
    args.push('-map', '0:v:0?', '-map', '0:a:0?');

    if (canCopyVideo) {
      args.push('-c:v', 'copy');
    } else {
      args.push(
        '-c:v', 'libx264',
        '-preset', 'ultrafast',
        '-tune', 'zerolatency',
        '-crf', '20',
        '-pix_fmt', 'yuv420p'
      );
    }

    args.push(
      '-c:a', 'aac',
      '-b:a', '192k',
      '-ac', '2',
      '-sn',
      '-dn',
      '-avoid_negative_ts', 'make_zero',
      '-threads', '0',
      '-f', 'mp4',
      '-movflags', 'frag_keyframe+empty_moov+default_base_moof',
      'pipe:1'
    );

    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'video/mp4',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
      'Cache-Control': 'no-store'
    });

    try {
      req.socket?.setNoDelay?.(true);
      res.socket?.setNoDelay?.(true);
    } catch {}

    const proc = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true });
    this.activeStreamProc = proc;

    proc.stdout.pipe(res);

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (this.activeStreamProc === proc) {
        this.activeStreamProc = null;
      }
      try {
        proc.kill('SIGKILL');
      } catch {}
    };

    req.on('close', cleanup);
    res.on('close', cleanup);
    proc.on('error', cleanup);
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming HTTP /api/thumb requests
   * Extracts instant on-demand JPEG frame for timeline hover preview
   * @param {URL} urlObj
   * @param {http.IncomingMessage} req
   * @param {http.ServerResponse} res
   * @param {Object} mediaService
   */
  handleThumbRequest(urlObj, req, res, mediaService) {
    const filePath = urlObj.searchParams.get('path') || '';
    const timeSec = Math.max(0, Number(urlObj.searchParams.get('t')) || 0);

    const ffmpegPath = mediaService.getFfmpegPath();
    if (!ffmpegPath || !filePath || !fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end();
      return;
    }

    const cacheKey = `${filePath}:${timeSec.toFixed(1)}`;
    if (this.thumbCache.has(cacheKey)) {
      const cachedBuf = this.thumbCache.get(cacheKey);
      this.thumbCache.delete(cacheKey);
      this.thumbCache.set(cacheKey, cachedBuf);

      res.writeHead(200, {
        'Content-Type': 'image/jpeg',
        'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
        'Cache-Control': 'max-age=180',
        'Content-Length': cachedBuf.length
      });
      res.end(cachedBuf);
      return;
    }

    this.killActiveThumbProc();

    const args = [
      '-hide_banner',
      '-loglevel', 'error',
      '-ss', timeSec.toFixed(2),
      '-i', filePath,
      '-an',
      '-sn',
      '-dn',
      '-frames:v', '1',
      '-vf', 'scale=320:-2',
      '-q:v', '5',
      '-threads', '2',
      '-f', 'image2pipe',
      '-vcodec', 'mjpeg',
      'pipe:1'
    ];

    res.writeHead(200, {
      'Content-Type': 'image/jpeg',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
      'Cache-Control': 'max-age=180'
    });

    const proc = spawn(ffmpegPath, args, { windowsHide: true });
    this.activeThumbProc = proc;

    const chunks = [];
    proc.stdout.on('data', (c) => chunks.push(c));
    proc.stdout.pipe(res);

    const cleanup = () => {
      if (this.activeThumbProc === proc) {
        this.activeThumbProc = null;
      }
      try {
        proc.kill('SIGKILL');
      } catch {}
    };

    req.on('close', cleanup);
    proc.on('close', () => {
      if (this.activeThumbProc === proc) {
        this.activeThumbProc = null;
      }
      if (chunks.length > 0) {
        const fullBuf = Buffer.concat(chunks);
        if (fullBuf.length > 80) {
          if (this.thumbCache.size >= MAX_THUMB_CACHE) {
            const oldestKey = this.thumbCache.keys().next().value;
            this.thumbCache.delete(oldestKey);
          }
          this.thumbCache.set(cacheKey, fullBuf);
        }
      }
    });
    proc.on('error', cleanup);
  }
}

function createStreamManager() {
  return new StreamProcessManager();
}

module.exports = {
  StreamProcessManager,
  createStreamManager
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/logger.js
// Structured, prefixed logging module for VLCopyCat Electron Main process

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

class Logger {
  constructor(scope = 'Main') {
    this.scope = scope;
    this.prefix = `[VLCopyCat:${scope}]`;
  }

  static getLogFile() {
    try {
      const electron = require('electron');
      const app = electron.app;
      const baseDir = app && typeof app.getPath === 'function' ? app.getPath('userData') : path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
      return path.join(baseDir, 'copycat.log');
    } catch {
      return path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'copycat.log');
    }
  }

  static writeToFile(level, scope, message, meta) {
    try {
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString();
      const metaStr = meta !== undefined && meta !== null
        ? (typeof meta === 'object' ? ` ${JSON.stringify(meta)}` : ` ${meta}`)
        : '';
      const line = `[${stamp}] [${level.toUpperCase()}] [${scope}] ${message}${metaStr}\n`;
      fs.appendFileSync(Logger.getLogFile(), line, 'utf8');
    } catch {
      // Safe fallback: never crash on file logging errors
    }
  }

  static formatTime() {
    const d = new Date();
    const h = String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
    const m = String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
    const s = String(d.getSeconds()).padStart(2, '0');
    const ms = String(d.getMilliseconds()).padStart(3, '0');
    return `${h}:${m}:${s}.${ms}`;
  }

  log(level, message, ...args) {
    const time = Logger.formatTime();
    const tag = `${time} ${this.prefix}`;
    const meta = args.length > 0 ? (args.length === 1 ? args[0] : args) : null;

    if (level === 'error') {
      console.error(tag, message, ...args);
      Logger.writeToFile('error', this.scope, message, meta);
    } else if (level === 'warn') {
      console.warn(tag, message, ...args);
      Logger.writeToFile('warn', this.scope, message, meta);
    } else if (level === 'debug') {
      if (process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
        console.debug(tag, message, ...args);
        Logger.writeToFile('debug', this.scope, message, meta);
      }
    } else {
      console.log(tag, message, ...args);
      if (process.env.COPYCAT_DEBUG) {
        Logger.writeToFile('info', this.scope, message, meta);
      }
    }
  }

  info(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('info', msg, ...args);
  }

  warn(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('warn', msg, ...args);
  }

  error(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('error', msg, ...args);
  }

  debug(msg, ...args) {
    this.log('debug', msg, ...args);
  }

  forScope(childScope) {
    return new Logger(childScope);
  }
}

const defaultLogger = new Logger('Main');

module.exports = {
  Logger,
  logger: defaultLogger,
  createLogger: (scope) => new Logger(scope)
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/session.js
// Windows Session Integrations, Taskbar Thumbar Management, Desktop Shortcuts, PiP Helpers & AI Sandbox Breakout

const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { spawn, spawnSync, execFile } = require('child_process');
const { app, nativeImage } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('Session');

/**
 * Auto-breakout for AI terminal sandboxes (Antigravity exebox, background runner sessions)
 * Ensures the Electron GUI spawns onto the physical user desktop rather than headless background tasks.
 */
function handleAiSandboxBreakout() {
  if (
    process.platform === 'win32' &&
    (process.env.ANTIGRAVITY_AGENT || process.env.AI_SANDBOX) &&
    !process.argv.includes('--desktop-spawned')
  ) {
    try {
      const isPackaged = !process.defaultApp && !/[\\/]electron[\\/]dist[\\/]electron\.exe$/i.test(process.execPath);
      const exe = process.execPath;
      const cleanArgs = process.argv
        .slice(1)
        .filter((a) => a !== '.' && !a.startsWith('--'))
        .map((a) => `\\"${a.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}\\"`)
        .join(' ');
      const safeExe = exe.replace(/'/g, "''");
      let cmd;
      if (isPackaged) {
        cmd = `$s = New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application; $s.ShellExecute('${safeExe}', ('--desktop-spawned ' + '${cleanArgs.replace(/'/g, "''")}').Trim(), '', 'open', 1)`;
      } else {
        const appRoot = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..').replace(/'/g, "''");
        cmd = `$s = New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application; $s.ShellExecute('${safeExe}', ('"${appRoot}" --desktop-spawned ' + '${cleanArgs.replace(/'/g, "''")}').Trim(), '', 'open', 1)`;
      }
      spawn('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-Command', cmd], {
        detached: true,
        stdio: 'ignore',
        windowsHide: true
      }).unref();
      logger.info('Sandbox breakout triggered successfully.');
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Sandbox breakout failed:', err.message);
    }
    process.exit(0);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Windows Native PiP Helper Binary
// ----------------------------------------------------
const PIP_HELPER_PATH = (() => {
  const packaged = path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', 'pip-helper.exe');
  if (fs.existsSync(packaged)) return packaged;
  return path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'bin', 'pip-helper.exe');
})();

function getMainHwndString(win) {
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return '0';
  try {
    const buf = win.getNativeWindowHandle();
    if (buf.length >= 8) return buf.readBigUInt64LE(0).toString();
    if (buf.length >= 4) return String(buf.readUInt32LE(0));
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('Failed to read native window handle:', err.message);
  }
  return '0';
}

function resizePipWindow(win, aspect = 16 / 9) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !fs.existsSync(PIP_HELPER_PATH)) {
      resolve({ success: false, bounds: null });
      return;
    }
    const hwndStr = getMainHwndString(win);
    const args = [
      String(process.pid),
      hwndStr,
      Number(aspect || 1.7778).toFixed(4),
      '0.28'
    ];
    execFile(PIP_HELPER_PATH, args, { windowsHide: true, timeout: 1500 }, (err, stdout) => {
      const out = String(stdout || '').trim();
      if (!err && out.startsWith('OK ')) {
        const bounds = out.slice(3);
        resolve({ success: true, bounds });
      } else {
        const bounds = out || (err ? err.message : 'ERR');
        resolve({ success: false, bounds });
      }
    });
  });
}

function restoreMainWindowWin32(win) {
  if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !fs.existsSync(PIP_HELPER_PATH)) return;
  const hwnd = getMainHwndString(win);
  if (hwnd && hwnd !== '0') {
    execFile(PIP_HELPER_PATH, ['--restore', hwnd], { windowsHide: true, timeout: 1000 }, () => {});
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Taskbar Thumbar Management
// ----------------------------------------------------
let currentThumbarPlaying = null;

function updateThumbarButtons(mainWindow, isPlaying = false) {
  if (process.platform !== 'win32' || !mainWindow || mainWindow.isDestroyed()) return;
  const normalizedPlaying = Boolean(isPlaying);
  if (currentThumbarPlaying === normalizedPlaying) return;
  currentThumbarPlaying = normalizedPlaying;

  const assetsDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'assets');
  const prevIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, 'thumbar-prev.png'));
  const playIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, normalizedPlaying ? 'thumbar-pause.png' : 'thumbar-play.png'));
  const nextIcon = nativeImage.createFromPath(path.join(assetsDir, 'thumbar-next.png'));

  try {
    mainWindow.setThumbarButtons([
      {
        tooltip: 'Previous Track',
        icon: prevIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'prev' });
          }
        }
      },
      {
        tooltip: normalizedPlaying ? 'Pause' : 'Play',
        icon: playIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'togglePlay' });
          }
        }
      },
      {
        tooltip: 'Next Track',
        icon: nextIcon,
        click() {
          if (mainWindow && !mainWindow.isDestroyed()) {
            mainWindow.webContents.send('ai:command', { action: 'next' });
          }
        }
      }
    ]);
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('Failed to set Thumbar buttons:', err.message);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Desktop Shortcut Management
// ----------------------------------------------------
function getDesktopShortcutPath() {
  const desktopPath = app.getPath('desktop');
  return path.join(desktopPath, 'CopyCat media player.lnk');
}

function hasDesktopShortcut() {
  try {
    return fs.existsSync(getDesktopShortcutPath());
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function createDesktopShortcut() {
  try {
    const rootDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
    const shortcutPath = getDesktopShortcutPath();
    const icoPath = path.join(rootDir, 'assets', 'icon.ico');
    const exeLauncher = path.join(rootDir, 'Launch_CopyCat.exe');
    const isPackaged = app.isPackaged;
    const targetPath = isPackaged 
      ? process.execPath 
      : (fs.existsSync(exeLauncher) ? exeLauncher : path.join(rootDir, 'Launch_CopyCat.bat'));
    const workingDir = isPackaged ? path.dirname(process.execPath) : rootDir;
    const iconLocation = fs.existsSync(icoPath) ? `${icoPath},0` : `${targetPath},0`;

    const psScript = `
      $ws = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell;
      $s = $ws.CreateShortcut('${shortcutPath.replace(/'/g, "''")}');
      $s.TargetPath = '${targetPath.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
      $s.WorkingDirectory = '${workingDir.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
      $s.IconLocation = '${iconLocation.replace(/'/g, "''")}';
      $s.Description = 'CopyCat media player';
      $s.Save();
    `;
    spawnSync('powershell.exe', ['-NoProfile', '-NonInteractive', '-Command', psScript], {
      windowsHide: true
    });
    const created = fs.existsSync(shortcutPath);
    logger.info(`Desktop shortcut creation result: ${created}`);
    return { success: created, ok: created, shortcutPath };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Failed to create desktop shortcut:', err.message);
    return { success: false, ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Windows File Associations & AI Integration
// ----------------------------------------------------
function getAssociationsScriptPath() {
  const rootDir = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..');
  const candidates = [
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', 'register_associations.ps1'),
    path.join(rootDir, 'bin', 'register_associations.ps1')
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (c && fs.existsSync(c)) return c;
  }
  return null;
}

function runAssociationsRegistration(targetExe = process.execPath) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const regScript = getAssociationsScriptPath();
      if (!regScript) {
        logger.warn('register_associations.ps1 script not found');
        return resolve({ ok: false, error: 'register_associations.ps1 script not found' });
      }
      const args = ['-NoProfile', '-ExecutionPolicy', 'Bypass', '-File', regScript];
      if (targetExe && typeof targetExe === 'string' && fs.existsSync(targetExe)) {
        args.push('-TargetExe', targetExe);
      }
      const child = spawn('powershell.exe', args, {
        windowsHide: true,
        stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe']
      });
      let stderr = '';
      child.stderr.on('data', (d) => { stderr += d.toString(); });
      child.on('close', (code) => {
        logger.info(`register_associations.ps1 finished with exit code ${code}`);
        resolve({ ok: code === 0, error: stderr });
      });
      child.on('error', (err) => {
        logger.error('Failed to run register_associations.ps1:', err.message);
        resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
      });
    } catch (err) {
      logger.error('Associations execution exception:', err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
    }
  });
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// Window Focus & Bring to Front Helper
// ----------------------------------------------------
function bringToFront(win, userAlwaysOnTop = false) {
  if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
  try {
    if (win.isMinimized()) win.restore();
    win.show();
    win.focus();
    if (!userAlwaysOnTop) {
      try {
        win.setAlwaysOnTop(true, 'screen-saver');
      } catch {
        win.setAlwaysOnTop(true);
      }
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed() && !userAlwaysOnTop) {
          win.setAlwaysOnTop(false);
        }
      }, 250);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug('bringToFront error:', err.message);
  }
}

module.exports = {
  handleAiSandboxBreakout,
  PIP_HELPER_PATH,
  getMainHwndString,
  resizePipWindow,
  restoreMainWindowWin32,
  updateThumbarButtons,
  getDesktopShortcutPath,
  hasDesktopShortcut,
  createDesktopShortcut,
  getAssociationsScriptPath,
  runAssociationsRegistration,
  bringToFront
};
``````

################################################################################
## DATEI: ./src/main/updater.js

``````javascript
// src/main/updater.js
// GitHub Releases Auto-Updater Engine for VLCopyCat

const https = require('https');
const http = require('http');
const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');
const os = require('os');
const { URL } = require('url');
const { spawn } = require('child_process');
const { shell, app } = require('electron');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('Updater');
const GITHUB_REPO = 'georgschneider96-collab/CopyCat-media-player';

/**
 * Compare semantic versions (vX.Y.Z)
 * @param {string} currentVer
 * @param {string} latestTag
 * @returns {boolean} True if latestTag is newer than currentVer
 */
function isNewerVersion(currentVer, latestTag) {
  const cleanCurr = (currentVer || '').replace(/^v/i, '').trim();
  const cleanLate = (latestTag || '').replace(/^v/i, '').trim();
  const pCurr = cleanCurr.split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const pLate = cleanLate.split('.').map((n) => parseInt(n, 10) || 0);

  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(pCurr.length, pLate.length); i++) {
    const c = pCurr[i] || 0;
    const l = pLate[i] || 0;
    if (l > c) return true;
    if (l < c) return false;
  }
  return false;
}

/**
 * Query GitHub Releases API for latest release info
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
function checkForUpdates() {
  const packageJsonPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'package.json');
  let currentVersion = '1.0.0';
  try {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(packageJsonPath, 'utf8'));
    currentVersion = pkg.version || '1.0.0';
  } catch {}

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const reqOptions = {
      hostname: 'api.github.com',
      path: `/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest`,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'CopyCat-Media-Player',
        'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json'
      }
    };

    const req = https.get(reqOptions, (res) => {
      let data = '';
      res.on('data', (chunk) => {
        data += chunk;
      });

      res.on('end', () => {
        try {
          if (res.statusCode !== 200) {
            logger.warn(`GitHub API returned status ${res.statusCode}`);
            return resolve({ ok: false, error: `GitHub API status ${res.statusCode}` });
          }

          const release = JSON.parse(data);
          const latestTag = release.tag_name || '';
          const isNewer = isNewerVersion(currentVersion, latestTag);

          let asset = null;
          if (Array.isArray(release.assets) && release.assets.length > 0) {
            asset = release.assets.find((a) => a.name.toLowerCase().includes('setup') && a.name.endsWith('.exe'))
              || release.assets.find((a) => a.name.endsWith('.exe'))
              || release.assets[0];
          }

          logger.info(`Update check: current=${currentVersion}, latest=${latestTag}, available=${isNewer}`);
          resolve({
            ok: true,
            updateAvailable: isNewer,
            currentVersion: `v${currentVersion.replace(/^v/i, '')}`,
            latestVersion: latestTag,
            releaseName: release.name || latestTag,
            releaseNotes: release.body || '',
            releaseUrl: release.html_url || '',
            asset: asset ? {
              name: asset.name,
              size: asset.size,
              downloadUrl: asset.browser_download_url
            } : null
          });
        } catch (err) {
          logger.error('Failed to parse update release response:', err.message);
          resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
        }
      });
    });

    req.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.warn('Update request network error:', err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, error: err.message });
    });

    req.setTimeout(8000, () => {
      req.destroy();
      logger.warn('Update check timed out after 8s');
      resolve({ ok: false, error: 'Request timed out' });
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Download update setup asset with redirect handling and progress events
 * @param {string} downloadUrl
 * @param {string} assetName
 * @param {Function} onProgress
 * @param {number} maxRedirects
 * @returns {Promise<string>} Downloaded file path
 */
function downloadUpdateAsset(downloadUrl, assetName, onProgress, maxRedirects = 6) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const destPath = path.join(os.tmpdir(), assetName || 'CopyCat-Update-Setup.exe');

    function get(currentUrl, redirectsLeft) {
      if (redirectsLeft <= 0) {
        return reject(new Error('Too many redirects while downloading update'));
      }

      const parsed = new URL(currentUrl);
      const proto = parsed.protocol === 'https:' ? https : http;

      proto.get({
        protocol: parsed.protocol,
        hostname: parsed.hostname,
        port: parsed.port,
        path: parsed.pathname + parsed.search,
        headers: {
          'User-Agent': 'CopyCat-Media-Player',
          'Accept': 'application/octet-stream'
        }
      }, (res) => {
        if (res.statusCode >= 300 && res.statusCode < 400 && res.headers.location) {
          const nextUrl = new URL(res.headers.location, currentUrl).href;
          return get(nextUrl, redirectsLeft - 1);
        }

        if (res.statusCode !== 200) {
          return reject(new Error(`Download failed with status ${res.statusCode}`));
        }

        const total = parseInt(res.headers['content-length'] || '0', 10);
        let transferred = 0;
        let lastReport = 0;
        const fileStream = fs.createWriteStream(destPath);

        res.on('data', (chunk) => {
          transferred += chunk.length;
          const now = Date.now();
          if (now - lastReport > 80 || transferred === total) {
            lastReport = now;
            const percent = total > 0 ? Math.round((transferred / total) * 100) : 0;
            if (onProgress) onProgress({ percent, transferred, total });
          }
        });

        res.pipe(fileStream);

        fileStream.on('finish', () => {
          fileStream.close(() => resolve(destPath));
        });

        fileStream.on('error', (err) => {
          try { fs.unlinkSync(destPath); } catch {}
          reject(err);
        });
      }).on('error', reject);
    }

    get(downloadUrl, maxRedirects);
  });
}

/**
 * Download update and launch the Windows installer
 * @param {Object} asset
 * @param {BrowserWindow} win
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
async function downloadAndInstallUpdate(asset, win) {
  if (!asset || !asset.downloadUrl) {
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:error', { error: 'No download asset available' });
    }
    return { ok: false, error: 'No download asset' };
  }

  try {
    logger.info(`Starting download of update asset: ${asset.name}`);
    const downloadedPath = await downloadUpdateAsset(
      asset.downloadUrl,
      asset.name,
      (progress) => {
        if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
          win.webContents.send('updater:downloadProgress', progress);
        }
      }
    );

    logger.info(`Download completed successfully: ${downloadedPath}`);
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:downloadComplete', { path: downloadedPath });
    }

    // Launch downloaded installer and quit current app instance
    setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        const child = spawn(downloadedPath, [], {
          detached: true,
          stdio: 'ignore'
        });
        child.unref();
      } catch (launchErr) {
        logger.warn('Direct launch failed, attempting shell.openPath:', launchErr.message);
        shell.openPath(downloadedPath);
      }
      app.quit();
    }, 1000);

    return { ok: true, path: downloadedPath };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.error('Update download or installation error:', err.message);
    if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
      win.webContents.send('updater:error', { error: err.message });
    }
    return { ok: false, error: err.message };
  }
}

module.exports = {
  GITHUB_REPO,
  isNewerVersion,
  checkForUpdates,
  downloadUpdateAsset,
  downloadAndInstallUpdate
};
``````

## DATEI: ./src/main/media-resolver.js
################################################################################

``````javascript
// src/main/media-resolver.js
// Media Detection, Header-Byte Sniffing, Codec Probing and File Indexing for VLCopyCat

const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { spawn, spawnSync } = require('child_process');
const { pathToFileURL } = require('url');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('MediaResolver');

// Recognized media file extensions
const MEDIA_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  '.mp4', '.mkv', '.webm', '.mov', '.avi', '.m4v', '.ogv',
  '.ts', '.m2ts', '.flv', '.wmv', '.mpg', '.mpeg', '.vob', '.3gp',
  '.mp3', '.flac', '.wav', '.ogg', '.m4a', '.aac', '.opus'
]);

// Dedicated audio formats
const AUDIO_EXTENSIONS = new Set([
  '.mp3', '.flac', '.wav', '.ogg', '.m4a', '.aac', '.opus'
]);

// Supported subtitle file formats
const SUB_EXTENSIONS = ['.srt', '.vtt'];

// Known ISO Base Media File Format box / atom types
const ISO_BMFF_ATOMS = new Set([
  'ftyp', 'moov', 'mdat', 'free', 'wide', 'skip', 'pnot', 'styp'
]);

/**
 * Locate FFmpeg & FFprobe binaries in package, local bin or system PATH
 * @param {string} toolName 'ffmpeg' or 'ffprobe'
 * @returns {string|null} Resolved executable path or null
 */
function resolveMediaTool(toolName) {
  const exe = process.platform === 'win32' ? `${toolName}.exe` : toolName;
  const candidates = [
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', 'bin', exe),
    path.join(__dirname, '..', '..', 'bin', exe),
    path.join(process.resourcesPath || '', exe)
  ];

  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (c && fs.existsSync(c)) {
      return c;
    }
  }

  try {
    const check = spawnSync(process.platform === 'win32' ? 'where.exe' : 'which', [toolName], {
      encoding: 'utf8',
      windowsHide: true
    });
    if (check.status === 0 && check.stdout) {
      const first = check.stdout.split(/\r?\n/).map((s) => s.trim()).find(Boolean);
      if (first && fs.existsSync(first)) {
        return first;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`Error checking PATH for ${toolName}:`, err.message);
  }

  return null;
}

const FFMPEG_PATH = resolveMediaTool('ffmpeg');
const FFPROBE_PATH = resolveMediaTool('ffprobe');

function hasFfmpeg() {
  return Boolean(FFMPEG_PATH);
}

function hasFfprobe() {
  return Boolean(FFPROBE_PATH);
}

/**
 * Fast 0.05ms header byte sniffer to catch MPEG-TS disguised as .mp4 and non-Chromium containers
 * @param {string} filePath Absolute path to media file
 * @param {string} ext Lowercase extension (.mp4, .mkv, etc.)
 * @returns {boolean} True if FFmpeg transcoding/remux stream is required
 */
function detectNeedsStream(filePath, ext) {
  if (!FFMPEG_PATH) return false;
  if (AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.has(ext)) return false;

  // Formats known to require remuxing/transcoding for Chromium video tag
  if (['.ts', '.m2ts', '.avi', '.flv', '.wmv', '.mpg', '.mpeg', '.vob'].includes(ext)) {
    return true;
  }

  let fd = null;
  try {
    fd = fs.openSync(filePath, 'r');
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(196);
    const bytesRead = fs.readSync(fd, buf, 0, 196, 0);
    if (bytesRead < 12) return false;

    // MPEG-TS sync byte 0x47 at offset 0 and offset 188 (common in HLS downloads saved as .mp4)
    if (buf[0] === 0x47 && (bytesRead < 189 || buf[188] === 0x47)) {
      return true;
    }

    // AVI ('RIFF'), FLV ('FLV')
    const head3 = buf.subarray(0, 3).toString('ascii');
    const head4 = buf.subarray(0, 4).toString('ascii');
    if (head4 === 'RIFF' || head3 === 'FLV') {
      return true;
    }

    // Verify ISO Base Media File Format atom for .mp4 / .m4v / .mov
    if (['.mp4', '.m4v', '.mov', '.3gp'].includes(ext)) {
      const atom = buf.subarray(4, 8).toString('ascii');
      if (!ISO_BMFF_ATOMS.has(atom)) {
        return true;
      }
    }
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`detectNeedsStream error on "${filePath}":`, err.message);
    return false;
  } finally {
    if (fd !== null) {
      try {
        fs.closeSync(fd);
      } catch {}
    }
  }

  return false;
}

/**
 * Deep media probe using ffprobe
 * Extracts video/audio codec, container duration, dimensions, and chapter marks
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @returns {Promise<Object>}
 */
function probeMediaFile(filePath) {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    if (!FFPROBE_PATH || !filePath || !fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      return resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
    }

    const args = [
      '-v', 'error',
      '-show_entries', 'format=format_name,duration',
      '-show_entries', 'stream=index,codec_type,codec_name,width,height',
      '-show_chapters',
      '-of', 'json',
      filePath
    ];

    const proc = spawn(FFPROBE_PATH, args, { windowsHide: true });
    let out = '';

    proc.stdout.on('data', (chunk) => {
      out += chunk.toString();
    });

    proc.on('error', (err) => {
      logger.warn(`ffprobe execution error for "${filePath}":`, err.message);
      resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
    });

    proc.on('close', () => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(out || '{}');
        const streams = parsed.streams || [];
        const vStream = streams.find((s) => s.codec_type === 'video');
        const aStream = streams.find((s) => s.codec_type === 'audio');
        const duration = Number(parsed.format?.duration) || 0;
        const rawChapters = Array.isArray(parsed.chapters) ? parsed.chapters : [];
        const chapters = rawChapters.map((c, idx) => ({
          index: idx,
          start: Math.max(0, Number(c.start_time) || (Number(c.start) / 1000) || 0),
          end: Math.max(0, Number(c.end_time) || (Number(c.end) / 1000) || 0),
          title: (c.tags && (c.tags.title || c.tags.TITLE || c.tags.Title)) || ''
        })).filter((c) => c.end > c.start);

        resolve({
          ok: true,
          formatName: parsed.format?.format_name || '',
          duration,
          videoCodec: vStream?.codec_name || null,
          audioCodec: aStream?.codec_name || null,
          width: vStream?.width || 0,
          height: vStream?.height || 0,
          chapters
        });
      } catch (parseErr) {
        logger.warn(`ffprobe output JSON parse error:`, parseErr.message);
        resolve({ ok: false, duration: 0, videoCodec: null, audioCodec: null, chapters: [] });
      }
    });
  });
}

/**
 * Natural alphabetical + numeric sorting comparison
 */
function naturalCompare(a, b) {
  return a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
}

/**
 * Automatically locate matching subtitle file in directory
 * @param {string} mediaFilePath
 * @param {Array|null} directoryEntries
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function findAutoSubtitle(mediaFilePath, directoryEntries = null) {
  try {
    const dir = path.dirname(mediaFilePath);
    const base = path.basename(mediaFilePath, path.extname(mediaFilePath));

    for (const ext of SUB_EXTENSIONS) {
      const candidate = path.join(dir, base + ext);
      if (fs.existsSync(candidate)) {
        return candidate;
      }
    }

    const entries = Array.isArray(directoryEntries) ? directoryEntries : fs.readdirSync(dir);
    const baseLower = base.toLowerCase() + '.';
    const match = entries.find((f) => {
      const name = typeof f === 'string' ? f : f?.name;
      if (!name) return false;
      const lower = name.toLowerCase();
      return (
        lower.startsWith(baseLower) &&
        SUB_EXTENSIONS.some((ext) => lower.endsWith(ext))
      );
    });

    if (match) {
      const matchName = typeof match === 'string' ? match : match.name;
      return path.join(dir, matchName);
    }
    return null;
  } catch (err) {
    logger.debug(`findAutoSubtitle error:`, err.message);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Generate privileged media:// custom protocol URL
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @returns {string}
 */
function toMediaUrl(filePath) {
  if (!filePath) return '';
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath);
  const normalized = resolved.replace(/\\/g, '/');
  return `media:///${encodeURI(normalized).replace(/#/g, '%23').replace(/\?/g, '%3F')}`;
}

/**
 * Construct fully resolved media item descriptor
 * @param {string} filePath
 * @param {Array|null} directoryEntries
 * @returns {Object}
 */
function buildMediaItem(filePath, directoryEntries = null) {
  const resolved = path.resolve(filePath);
  const ext = path.extname(resolved).toLowerCase();
  const isAudio = AUDIO_EXTENSIONS.has(ext);
  const folderPath = path.dirname(resolved);
  const autoSubPath = findAutoSubtitle(resolved, directoryEntries);
  const needsStream = detectNeedsStream(resolved, ext);

  return {
    filePath: resolved,
    fileUrl: toMediaUrl(resolved),
    rawFileUrl: pathToFileURL(resolved).href,
    name: path.basename(resolved),
    title: path.basename(resolved, path.extname(resolved)),
    ext,
    isAudio,
    needsStream,
    folderPath,
    folderName: path.basename(folderPath) || folderPath,
    autoSubPath
  };
}

/**
 * Scan directory recursively for media items and subdirectories
 * @param {string} folderPath
 * @returns {Object}
 */
function scanMediaFolder(folderPath) {
  try {
    const resolvedDir = path.resolve(folderPath);
    const entries = fs.readdirSync(resolvedDir, { withFileTypes: true });

    const subfolders = entries
      .filter((d) => d.isDirectory() && !d.name.startsWith('.') && !d.name.startsWith('$'))
      .map((d) => d.name)
      .sort(naturalCompare)
      .map((name) => ({
        name,
        folderPath: path.join(resolvedDir, name)
      }));

    const files = entries
      .filter((d) => d.isFile() && MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.has(path.extname(d.name).toLowerCase()))
      .map((d) => d.name)
      .sort(naturalCompare)
      .map((name) => buildMediaItem(path.join(resolvedDir, name), entries));

    const parentDir = path.dirname(resolvedDir);

    return {
      folderPath: resolvedDir,
      parentPath: parentDir !== resolvedDir ? parentDir : null,
      folderName: path.basename(resolvedDir) || resolvedDir,
      subfolders,
      items: files
    };
  } catch (err) {
    logger.warn(`Failed to scan media folder "${folderPath}":`, err.message);
    return {
      folderPath,
      parentPath: null,
      folderName: path.basename(folderPath || ''),
      subfolders: [],
      items: [],
      error: err.message
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Extract media item and sibling folder from command line arguments
 * @param {Array} argv
 * @returns {Object|null}
 */
function resolveCliMediaFile(argv) {
  if (!Array.isArray(argv)) return null;
  const match = argv.slice(1).find((arg) => {
    if (!arg || typeof arg !== 'string') return false;
    const clean = arg.replace(/^"|"$/g, '').trim();
    if (!clean || clean.startsWith('-') || clean === '.') return false;
    const ext = path.extname(clean).toLowerCase();
    return MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.has(ext) && fs.existsSync(clean);
  });

  if (match) {
    const clean = match.replace(/^"|"$/g, '').trim();
    const item = buildMediaItem(clean);
    const sibling = scanMediaFolder(item.folderPath);
    return { item, folder: sibling };
  }

  return null;
}

module.exports = {
  MEDIA_EXTENSIONS,
  AUDIO_EXTENSIONS,
  SUB_EXTENSIONS,
  ISO_BMFF_ATOMS,
  FFMPEG_PATH,
  FFPROBE_PATH,
  hasFfmpeg,
  hasFfprobe,
  resolveMediaTool,
  detectNeedsStream,
  probeMediaFile,
  naturalCompare,
  findAutoSubtitle,
  toMediaUrl,
  buildMediaItem,
  scanMediaFolder,
  resolveCliMediaFile
};
``````

``````javascript
// src/main/ipc.js
// Declarative IPC Registry with Dependency Injection and Defensive Validation for VLCopyCat

const { ipcMain, dialog, shell, clipboard, nativeImage, app } = require('electron');
const path = require('path');
const fs = require('fs');
const { createLogger } = require('./logger');

const logger = createLogger('IPC');

/**
 * Defensive path validation helpers
 */
function isValidPathString(p) {
  return typeof p === 'string' && p.trim().length > 0;
}

function pathExists(p) {
  try {
    return isValidPathString(p) && fs.existsSync(p);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Declarative IPC Handlers Map
 * Each handler receives injected services: { window, media, session, updater }
 */
const IPC_HANDLERS = {
  // ==========================================
  // Window Controls (Injected: window)
  // ==========================================
  'win:minimize': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Minimize main window',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
      if (win.isFullScreen()) {
        win.setFullScreen(false);
      }
      win.minimize();
      window.broadcastState();
    }
  },

  'win:maximize': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Toggle window maximize/unmaximize',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
      if (win.isFullScreen()) {
        win.setFullScreen(false);
      } else if (win.isMaximized()) {
        win.unmaximize();
      } else {
        win.maximize();
      }
      window.broadcastState();
    }
  },

  'win:toggleFullscreen': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Toggle fullscreen mode',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
      win.setFullScreen(!win.isFullScreen());
      window.broadcastState();
    }
  },

  'win:exitFullscreenToDrag': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Exit fullscreen to drag coordinates',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, data = {}) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return null;

      const screenX = Number(data.screenX) || 0;
      const screenY = Number(data.screenY) || 0;
      const ratioX = typeof data.ratioX === 'number' && data.ratioX >= 0 && data.ratioX <= 1 ? data.ratioX : 0.5;

      if (win.isFullScreen()) {
        win.setFullScreen(false);
      }
      if (win.isMaximized()) {
        win.unmaximize();
      }

      let width = 1180;
      let height = 700;
      try {
        const normal = win.getNormalBounds();
        if (normal && normal.width && normal.height) {
          width = normal.width;
          height = normal.height;
        }
      } catch {
        const bounds = win.getBounds();
        width = bounds.width || 1180;
        height = bounds.height || 700;
      }

      const targetX = Math.round(screenX - width * ratioX);
      const targetY = Math.round(screenY - 20);

      win.setBounds({
        x: targetX,
        y: targetY,
        width,
        height
      });

      window.broadcastState();
      return { x: targetX, y: targetY, width, height };
    }
  },

  'win:dragMove': {
    type: 'both',
    description: 'Move window to specified coordinates',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, coords = {}) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (!win || win.isDestroyed()) return;
      const x = Number(coords.x);
      const y = Number(coords.y);
      if (!Number.isNaN(x) && !Number.isNaN(y)) {
        win.setPosition(Math.round(x), Math.round(y));
      }
    }
  },

  'win:toggleAlwaysOnTop': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Toggle always on top state',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      return window.applyAlwaysOnTop(!window.getUserAlwaysOnTop());
    }
  },

  'win:setAlwaysOnTop': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Explicitly set always on top flag',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, flag) => {
      return window.applyAlwaysOnTop(Boolean(flag));
    }
  },

  'win:enterPip': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Enter Picture-in-Picture mode',
    handler: async ({ window }, _evt, opts = {}) => {
      const aspect = Number(opts?.aspect) > 0.1 ? Number(opts.aspect) : 16 / 9;
      return window.enterPip(aspect);
    }
  },

  'win:leavePip': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Leave Picture-in-Picture mode',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, opts = {}) => {
      return window.leavePip(opts);
    }
  },

  'win:close': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Close main window',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        win.close();
      }
    }
  },

  'win:setOpacity': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Set window opacity (clamped 0.2..1.0)',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, opacity) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      if (win && !win.isDestroyed()) {
        const val = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(1.0, Number(opacity) || 1.0));
        win.setOpacity(val);
        return val;
      }
      return 1.0;
    }
  },

  'win:getState': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Get live window status',
    handler: ({ window, media }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      return {
        maximized: win?.isMaximized() || false,
        fullscreen: win?.isFullScreen() || false,
        minimized: win?.isMinimized() || false,
        alwaysOnTop: window.getUserAlwaysOnTop(),
        aiPort: window.getPort(),
        hasFfmpeg: media.hasFfmpeg()
      };
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Clipboard
  // ==========================================
  'clipboard:writeImage': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Copy base64 image data to clipboard',
    validate: (dataUrl) => typeof dataUrl === 'string' && dataUrl.startsWith('data:image/'),
    handler: (_deps, _evt, dataUrl) => {
      try {
        const img = nativeImage.createFromDataURL(dataUrl);
        clipboard.writeImage(img);
        return true;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error('Failed to copy image to clipboard:', err.message);
        return false;
      }
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Media & Streaming (Injected: media, window)
  // ==========================================
  'media:probe': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Probe media file with ffprobe',
    validate: (filePath) => isValidPathString(filePath),
    handler: async ({ media }, _evt, filePath) => {
      return media.probe(filePath);
    }
  },

  'media:stopStream': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Kill active FFmpeg stream and thumbnail worker processes',
    handler: ({ stream, window }) => {
      if (stream) {
        stream.killAll();
      } else {
        window?.killActiveStreamProc?.();
        window?.killActiveThumbProc?.();
      }
      return { ok: true };
    }
  },

  'media:getInitial': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Retrieve pending initial media launched via CLI or association',
    handler: ({ window }) => {
      return window.getInitialMedia();
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Native File/Folder Dialogs (Injected: window, media)
  // ==========================================
  'dialog:openFiles': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Open native file picker dialog for media',
    handler: async ({ window, media }, _evt, multi = false) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
        title: multi ? 'Open Multiple Media Files' : 'Open Media File',
        properties: multi ? ['openFile', 'multiSelections'] : ['openFile'],
        filters: [
          {
            name: 'Media Files',
            extensions: Array.from(media.MEDIA_EXTENSIONS).map((e) => e.slice(1))
          },
          { name: 'All Files', extensions: ['*'] }
        ]
      });

      if (result.canceled || !result.filePaths.length) return null;
      const items = result.filePaths.map((p) => media.buildItem(p));
      const folder = media.scanFolder(items[0].folderPath);
      return { items, folder };
    }
  },

  'dialog:openFolder': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Open native directory picker dialog',
    handler: async ({ window, media }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
        title: 'Open Media Directory',
        properties: ['openDirectory']
      });

      if (result.canceled || !result.filePaths.length) return null;
      return media.scanFolder(result.filePaths[0]);
    }
  },

  'dialog:openSubtitle': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Open native file picker for subtitle file (.srt, .vtt)',
    handler: async ({ window }) => {
      const win = window.getWindow();
      const result = await dialog.showOpenDialog(win, {
        title: 'Load Subtitle File (.srt, .vtt)',
        properties: ['openFile'],
        filters: [{ name: 'Subtitles', extensions: ['srt', 'vtt'] }]
      });

      if (result.canceled || !result.filePaths.length) return null;
      const subPath = result.filePaths[0];
      const content = fs.readFileSync(subPath, 'utf8');
      return { filePath: subPath, name: path.basename(subPath), content };
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Filesystem Operations (Injected: media)
  // ==========================================
  'fs:resolvePaths': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Resolve incoming dropped files/directories and extract media items',
    handler: ({ media }, _evt, paths) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(paths)) return null;
      const valid = paths.filter((p) => pathExists(p));
      if (!valid.length) return null;

      const collectedMediaItems = [];
      let primaryFolder = null;

      for (const p of valid) {
        try {
          const st = fs.statSync(p);
          if (st.isDirectory()) {
            const scanned = media.scanFolder(p);
            if (!primaryFolder) primaryFolder = scanned;
            if (scanned.items && scanned.items.length) {
              collectedMediaItems.push(...scanned.items);
            }
          } else if (st.isFile() && media.MEDIA_EXTENSIONS.has(path.extname(p).toLowerCase())) {
            collectedMediaItems.push(media.buildItem(p));
          }
        } catch (err) {
          logger.warn(`Error resolving path "${p}":`, err.message);
        }
      }

      const subPaths = valid.filter((p) => media.SUB_EXTENSIONS.includes(path.extname(p).toLowerCase()));
      if (!collectedMediaItems.length && subPaths.length) {
        return {
          subtitleOnly: {
            filePath: subPaths[0],
            name: path.basename(subPaths[0]),
            content: fs.readFileSync(subPaths[0], 'utf8')
          }
        };
      }

      if (!collectedMediaItems.length) return null;
      if (!primaryFolder) {
        primaryFolder = media.scanFolder(collectedMediaItems[0].folderPath);
      }
      return { items: collectedMediaItems, folder: primaryFolder };
    }
  },

  'fs:scanFolder': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Scan directory for media items and subfolders',
    validate: (folderPath) => pathExists(folderPath),
    handler: ({ media }, _evt, folderPath) => {
      return media.scanFolder(folderPath);
    }
  },

  'fs:readSubtitle': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Read UTF-8 content of subtitle file',
    validate: (subPath) => pathExists(subPath),
    handler: (_deps, _evt, subPath) => {
      try {
        return {
          filePath: subPath,
          name: path.basename(subPath),
          content: fs.readFileSync(subPath, 'utf8')
        };
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn(`Failed to read subtitle "${subPath}":`, err.message);
        return null;
      }
    }
  },

  'fs:getQuickFolders': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Retrieve standard OS user folders for media quick navigation',
    handler: () => {
      return [
        { label: 'Videos', path: app.getPath('videos') },
        { label: 'Desktop', path: app.getPath('desktop') },
        { label: 'Downloads', path: app.getPath('downloads') },
        { label: 'Music', path: app.getPath('music') }
      ];
    }
  },

  'fs:saveSnapshot': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Save player snapshot as PNG file to Pictures folder',
    validate: (payload) => payload && typeof payload.dataUrl === 'string' && payload.dataUrl.startsWith('data:image/'),
    handler: (_deps, _evt, { dataUrl, baseName }) => {
      try {
        const picturesDir = path.join(app.getPath('pictures'), 'CopyCat media player Snapshots');
        fs.mkdirSync(picturesDir, { recursive: true });
        const safe = (baseName || 'snapshot').replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]+/g, '_');
        const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
        const outPath = path.join(picturesDir, `${safe}_${stamp}.png`);
        const base64 = dataUrl.replace(/^data:image\/png;base64,/, '');
        fs.writeFileSync(outPath, Buffer.from(base64, 'base64'));
        logger.info(`Snapshot saved to: ${outPath}`);
        return { ok: true, outPath };
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error('Failed to save snapshot:', err.message);
        return { ok: false, error: err.message };
      }
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Desktop Shortcuts (Injected: session)
  // ==========================================
  'shortcut:hasDesktop': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Check if desktop shortcut exists',
    handler: ({ session }) => {
      return session.hasShortcut();
    }
  },

  'shortcut:createDesktop': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Create Windows desktop shortcut',
    handler: ({ session }) => {
      return session.createShortcut();
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // OS & App Integration (Injected: session)
  // ==========================================
  'app:setDefaultPlayer': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Register file associations for CopyCat media player',
    handler: ({ session }) => {
      return session.registerAssociations(process.execPath);
    }
  },

  'app:installAiIntegration': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Install Antigravity skill & PATH commands',
    handler: ({ session }) => {
      return session.registerAssociations(process.execPath);
    }
  },

  'app:openDefaultAppsSettings': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Open Windows 10/11 Default Apps system settings',
    handler: () => {
      try {
        shell.openExternal('ms-settings:defaultapps');
        return true;
      } catch (err) {
        logger.warn('Failed to open ms-settings:defaultapps:', err.message);
        return false;
      }
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // GitHub Auto-Updater (Injected: updater, window)
  // ==========================================
  'updater:check': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Check GitHub releases for updates',
    handler: ({ updater }) => {
      return updater.check();
    }
  },

  'updater:downloadAndInstall': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Download and execute latest release installer',
    handler: ({ updater, window }, _evt, asset) => {
      return updater.downloadAndInstall(asset, window.getWindow());
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Shell Utilities
  // ==========================================
  'shell:reveal': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Reveal item in Windows Explorer',
    validate: (filePath) => pathExists(filePath),
    handler: (_deps, _evt, filePath) => {
      shell.showItemInFolder(filePath);
      return true;
    }
  },

  'shell:openExternal': {
    type: 'handle',
    description: 'Open external URL in default web browser',
    validate: (targetUrl) => typeof targetUrl === 'string' && (targetUrl.startsWith('https://') || targetUrl.startsWith('http://')),
    handler: (_deps, _evt, targetUrl) => {
      shell.openExternal(targetUrl);
      return true;
    }
  },

  // ==========================================
  // Player Status Sync (Injected: window)
  // ==========================================
  'player:statusUpdate': {
    type: 'on',
    description: 'Receive playback telemetry from renderer and update Taskbar thumbar',
    handler: ({ window }, _evt, status) => {
      if (status && typeof status === 'object') {
        window.setStatus(status);
        if (typeof status.playing === 'boolean') {
          window.updateThumbar(status.playing);
        }
      }
    }
  }
};

/**
 * Register all declarative IPC handlers using injected services
 * @param {Object|ServiceContainer} containerOrServices
 */
function registerIpcRegistry(containerOrServices) {
  // Extract typed services from container or object
  const services = {
    window: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('window')
      ? containerOrServices.get('window')
      : containerOrServices.window,
    stream: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('stream')
      ? containerOrServices.get('stream')
      : containerOrServices.stream,
    media: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('media')
      ? containerOrServices.get('media')
      : containerOrServices.media,
    session: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('session')
      ? containerOrServices.get('session')
      : containerOrServices.session,
    updater: containerOrServices.has && containerOrServices.has('updater')
      ? containerOrServices.get('updater')
      : containerOrServices.updater
  };

  logger.info(`Registering ${Object.keys(IPC_HANDLERS).length} declarative IPC channels with injected services...`);

  for (const [channel, def] of Object.entries(IPC_HANDLERS)) {
    const wrappedHandler = async (evt, ...args) => {
      try {
        if (typeof def.validate === 'function') {
          const isValid = def.validate(...args);
          if (!isValid) {
            logger.warn(`Validation failed for IPC channel "${channel}" with args:`, args);
            return null;
          }
        }
        return await def.handler(services, evt, ...args);
      } catch (err) {
        logger.error(`Error in IPC handler "${channel}":`, err.stack || err.message);
        return { ok: false, error: err.message };
      }
    };

    if (def.type === 'handle' || def.type === 'both') {
      try {
        ipcMain.removeHandler(channel);
      } catch {}
      ipcMain.handle(channel, wrappedHandler);
    }

    if (def.type === 'on' || def.type === 'both') {
      ipcMain.removeAllListeners(channel);
      ipcMain.on(channel, (evt, ...args) => {
        wrappedHandler(evt, ...args).catch(() => {});
      });
    }
  }

  logger.info('Declarative IPC Registry successfully initialized with dependency injection.');
}

module.exports = {
  IPC_HANDLERS,
  registerIpcRegistry
};
``````
